# MotoCam — whole-project scan (2026-10-20)

Snapshot audit of commit `a33a583` (branch `main`). Every finding below was independently re-verified against the code: critical/high/medium findings by two separate verifiers (a code-truth lens and a reachability/impact lens), low/info findings by one combined verifier. **All 154 findings were confirmed; none were refuted.**

The *Status* column reflects the working tree after the fix phase (2026-10-21). Items marked *deferred* or *owner decision* were intentionally left unchanged.

## Executive summary

- **Scope.** 154 findings, every one confirmed by independent verifiers: 5 critical, 16 high, 55 medium, 74 low and 4 info.
- **Fix phase (working tree as of 2026-10-21).** 116 fixed, 23 partially fixed and 7 fixed in the documentation; 3 are deferred by the owner and 5 wait on an owner decision. See [Fix status](#fix-status) and the *Status* column.
- **Critical findings.** [F001](#f001) (Android 14+ foreground-service crash), [F002](#f002) (iOS Info.plist fragment), [F003](#f003) (iOS permission macros) and [F005](#f005) (retention deleted locked footage of other rides) are fixed. [F004](#f004), recording stopping when the screen locks or the app leaves the foreground without PiP, was deferred in the fix phase and has since been implemented (2026-10-28, see its fix note), but not yet confirmed on a device: while the recording's foreground service runs, MainActivity holds the lifecycle CameraX is bound to at STARTED, and a keep-screen-on setting (on by default) is the fallback.
- **Security and privacy.** The unused Mappls credential files are deleted from the tree and ignored, and they remain in the public git history, but the owner has already rotated those keys (confirmed 2026-10-21). Background location is no longer requested, recordings are excluded from Android backup and device transfer, gallery export of locked clips is opt-in, and the integrity envelope is described as what it is: an unkeyed SHA-256 integrity check that detects accidental corruption and naive edits, not tampering.
- **Owner decisions still open.** The application ID `com.example.motocam` cannot be published on Google Play ([migration steps](../release.md#application-id)); the published v0.1.5 to v0.1.12 APKs are debug-signed ([details](../release.md#signing-of-published-apks)); recordings storage location; a camera-plugin fork for Android bitrate and audio-off; the iOS iCloud backup policy; keyed integrity; a global storage budget.

### Tooling after the fix phase

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| `flutter analyze` | No issues, with 7 stricter lint rules added |
| `flutter test` | 222 of 222 passed (11 before) |
| `dart format --set-exit-if-changed lib test` | Clean |
| `scripts/release-security-precheck.ps1 -SkipTests -SkipAnalyze` | Passed |
| `flutter build apk --debug` | Succeeded in 6 min 43 s (AGP 8.11.1, Kotlin Gradle plugin 2.2.20, camera_android_camerax 0.7.2 with CameraX 1.6.0). One warning: the `jni` plugin, new through path_provider_android 2.3.1, builds with NDK 28.2.13676358 while the app pins 28.1.13356709; it is harmless |

Nothing has been run on a device or an emulator, and nothing for iOS has been built (no macOS). Device checks still needed: entering and leaving PiP and tapping the notification; the chronometer and "stopping" notifications; START with audio off on Android 14+; notification Stop with the screen locked; returning from system settings; gallery export on Android 9 and lower.

## Method

1. **Understand** — 9 readers mapped every subsystem line by line (camera, recording UI, playback, ride data and integrity, telemetry, settings/bootstrap, native bridges, maps, build/release/CI).
2. **Scan** — 10 dimension scanners (above) produced 207 evidence-backed findings from the maps, the source and 270 reader leads; tooling ran on the same tree.
3. **Dedup** — clustered into 154 canonical findings.
4. **Verify** — every finding re-checked by independent verifiers; severities corrected where the impact was over- or under-stated.

### Tooling baseline (before fixes)

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| `flutter analyze` | No issues (default `flutter_lints` only) |
| `flutter test` | 11/11 passed |
| `dart format --set-exit-if-changed` | 15 of 25 files not formatted |
| `flutter pub outdated` | 10 direct dependencies behind; `permission_handler` 13 and `latlong2` 0.10 majors available |
| `scripts/release-security-precheck.ps1` | Passed |
| `flutter build apk --debug` | Succeeded (Flutter 3.41.2, AGP 8.11.1, Kotlin 2.1.0, SDK 36, NDK 28.1) |

## Findings

### Critical (5)

#### F001

**On Android 14+ the two-argument startForeground() applies the manifest's camera\|microphone\|location types, so START crashes with a SecurityException when audio is off and RECORD_AUDIO was never granted**

Severity: **critical** · Category: android-platform · Location: `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/BackgroundRecordingService.kt:43` · Verification: two-lens (code-truth + reachability/impact) → confirmed · Status: **fixed**

Also affects: `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:63`, `lib/core/services/app_permission_service.dart:80`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:373`, `lib/features/recording/recording_screen.dart:414`, `lib/features/recording/widgets/recording_settings_sheet.dart:524`, `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/BackgroundRecordingService.kt:66`, `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/BackgroundRecordingService.kt:43`, `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:59-63` …

- **What is wrong:** onStartCommand(ACTION_START) calls the two-argument startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification), so the system applies every type declared on the service, android:foregroundServiceType='camera|microphone|location' (AndroidManifest.xml:63). For targetSdk 34+ (here 36) the system validates each type's runtime prerequisite, and the microphone type requires RECORD_AUDIO. The app deliberately supports recording without the microphone: the audit and CameraProvider request it only when audio is enabled (app_permission_service.dart:80-82; camera_provider.dart:373-375), and the preflight checks it only when audio is on (recording_screen.dart:590-603). The service starts after the camera is already recording (recording_screen.dart:403, then 414-416). The SecurityException is thrown inside Service.onStartCommand on the main thread, so it kills the process.
- **Failure scenario:** Preconditions: Android 14+ device, 'Record audio' switched off (recording_settings_sheet.dart:524-534), RECORD_AUDIO never granted or denied. The rider taps START RECORDING. The camera starts, startForegroundRecording runs, startForeground throws SecurityException, and the app dies about 1 s into the ride. The in-progress segment stays as a 0-byte file in the cache dir and nothing reaches 'MotoCam Recordings'. No telemetry is written. START_STICKY restarts an empty service process. Every START by such a user crashes.
- **Evidence:** Code: `startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification(elapsedMs))` (BackgroundRecordingService.kt:43); `android:foregroundServiceType="camera|microphone|location"` (AndroidManifest.xml:63); `if (audioEnabled) { await ensure(Permission.microphone, ...) }` (app_permission_service.dart:80-82). Reproduced on the MotoCam_API_36 emulator (Android 16) with a debug build of HEAD a33a583. Setup: audio disabled in prefs, RECORD_AUDIO not granted, all other permissions granted. Log: `20:36:47.258 [MotoCam] Recording started successfully`, then `20:36:48.079 E AndroidRuntime: FATAL EXCEPTION: main ... Unable to start service com.example.motocam.BackgroundRecordingService ... java.lang.SecurityException: Starting FGS with type microphone callerApp=ProcessRecord{...com.example.motocam/u0a216} targetSDK=36 requires permissions: all of the permissions allOf=true [android.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVI…
- **Verifier corrections:** The crash path needs audio switched off (non-default) and RECORD_AUDIO not granted. Camera and location are not exposure points because the audit blocks START without them. ServiceCompat.startForeground with a type mask requires androidx.core 1.12+. The app has no explicit androidx.core dependency (build.gradle.kts:106-107 lists only concurrent-futures), so it relies on the transitive version from the Flutter embedding. Calling Service.startForeground(id, n, type), gated on API 29+, avoids that dependency. Moving the FGS start before camera.startRecording() is optional: it changes start ordering. | This only affects devices running Android 14+ (API 34+). Older devices do not validate type p…
- **Recommendation:** In BackgroundRecordingService.onStartCommand(ACTION_START), compute the type mask: CAMERA, plus LOCATION if fine or coarse location is granted, plus MICROPHONE only if RECORD_AUDIO is granted (optionally also gated on an audioEnabled extra sent from MainActivity). Call ServiceCompat.startForeground(this, NOTIFICATION_ID, n, mask) inside a try/catch for SecurityException and ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException that releases the wake lock and calls stopSelf. Have the Dart side surface the failure instead of crashing.
- **Fix note:** FGS types from granted permissions; API 34+ zero-mask guard

#### F002

**ios/Runner/Info.plist is a fragment missing CFBundleIdentifier/Executable/PackageType/version, launch-screen, main-storyboard and scene-manifest keys, so the Runner app cannot be installed or launched**

Severity: **critical** · Category: ios-platform · Location: `ios/Runner/Info.plist:4` · Verification: two-lens (code-truth + reachability/impact) → confirmed · Status: **fixed**

Also affects: `ios/Runner.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj:549`, `ios/Runner.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj:571`, `ios/Runner.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj:370`, `ios/Runner/SceneDelegate.swift:4`, `ios/Runner/AppDelegate.swift:13`, `ios/Runner/Base.lproj/Main.storyboard:11`, `README.md:10`, `README.md:184` …

- **What is wrong:** The Runner target takes its Info.plist from ios/Runner/Info.plist. INFOPLIST_FILE = Runner/Info.plist is set and GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE is not (project.pbxproj:370/549/571); only the RunnerTests configurations set GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE = YES (:389/406/421). The file holds only five usage strings and UIBackgroundModes (Info.plist:5-24). Commit 95a364b replaced the Flutter template plist with a fragment, and no later commit restored the template keys. Compared with the Flutter 3.41.2 template (<flutter>/packages/flutter_tools/templates/app/ios.tmpl/Runner/Info.plist.tmpl:11-68), these keys are missing: - CFBundleIdentifier, CFBundleExecutable, CFBundlePackageType, CFBundleName - CFBundleShortVersionString, CFBundleVersion - LSRequiresIPhoneOS, UILaunchStoryboardName, UIMainStoryboardFile - UIApplicationSceneManifest, UISupportedInterfaceOrientations Consequences: 1. Xcode's Info.plist processor (swift-build) injects default bundle keys only when GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE is YES or an internal feature flag is set, and that flag defaults to off. The built Runner.app/Info.plist therefore has no identifier, executable, package type or version. 2. Neither FlutterAppDelegate nor FlutterS…
- **Failure scenario:** A contributor on macOS clones the repo and runs `flutter run -d <iPhone simulator>` as README.md:140 instructs. 1. xcodebuild produces Runner.app. ValidateProduct only warns about orientations and the launch storyboard. 2. The processed Info.plist has no CFBundleIdentifier, CFBundleExecutable or CFBundleVersion. 3. Install or launch fails. flutter_tools reads the bundle id from the built plist, prints 'Invalid prebuilt iOS app. Info.plist does not contain bundle identifier' and returns LaunchResult.failed(). A device install is expected to be refused as well (not verified on hardware). 4. `flutter build ipa` validation reports Version Number, Build Number and Bundle Identifier as 'Missing' and says 'You must set up the missing app settings.' App Store Connect would refuse the bundle for t…
- **Evidence:** - ios/Runner/Info.plist:4-25: the only keys are NSCameraUsageDescription, NSMicrophoneUsageDescription, NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription, NSLocationAlwaysAndWhenInUseUsageDescription, NSMotionUsageDescription and UIBackgroundModes [location, audio]. - project.pbxproj:549 `INFOPLIST_FILE = Runner/Info.plist;` (also :370, :571). The Runner configurations have no GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE. - swift-build (swiftlang/swift-build @a353ec9, 2026-06-11), Sources/SWBTaskExecution/TaskActions/InfoPlistProcessorTaskAction.swift:202: `if SWBFeatureFlag.enableDefaultInfoPlistTemplateKeys.value || scope.evaluate(BuiltinMacros.GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE) {` gates defaultInfoPlistContent (:695). That function adds CFBundleExecutable, CFBundleVersion, CFBundleIdentifier, CFBundlePackageType and CFBundleShortVersionString (:818-826). - Sources/SWBCore/SWBFeatureFlag.swift:49 `defaultValue: Bool = false`. :8…
- **Verifier corrections:** Minor: the claim that the pubspec version never reaches iOS is overstated as written. project.pbxproj:368/547/569 does reference $(FLUTTER_BUILD_NUMBER) through CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION. Without CFBundleVersion/CFBundleShortVersionString in the plist, though, it still does not land in the bundle, so the conclusion holds. The swift-build and engine-internal citations (FlutterViewController.mm, FlutterAppDelegate.mm) were not re-verified locally, but the central claim does not depend on them. | - The default-key gate in swift-build is confirmed against upstream main. The flutter_tools 'Invalid prebuilt iOS app' message and the App Store Connect rejection are cited from the finding and were not…
- **Recommendation:** Replace ios/Runner/Info.plist with the Flutter 3.41 template (packages/flutter_tools/templates/app/ios.tmpl/Runner/Info.plist.tmpl, with display names substituted). Re-add the NSCamera, NSMicrophone, NSLocationWhenInUse and NSMotion usage strings and UIBackgroundModes [location], dropping audio per F028. Until a macOS CI smoke build (`flutter build ios --simulator --no-codesign`) passes, mark iOS in README.md as unverified.
- **Fix note:** Info.plist rebuilt from Flutter 3.41.2 template

#### F003

**No tracked Podfile defines PERMISSION_CAMERA/MICROPHONE/LOCATION, so permission_handler_apple compiles those strategies out and iOS always reports permanently denied, despite README listing iOS as supported**

Severity: **critical** · Category: ios-platform · Location: `lib/core/services/app_permission_service.dart:78` · Verification: two-lens (code-truth + reachability/impact) → confirmed · Status: **fixed**

Also affects: `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:372`, `lib/features/recording/recording_screen.dart:97`, `lib/features/recording/recording_screen.dart:368`, `pubspec.yaml:28`, `README.md:161`, `README.md:10`, `README.md:7`, `README.md:123` …

- **What is wrong:** permission_handler_apple 9.4.7 (resolved in pubspec.lock) compiles every iOS permission out unless the app's Podfile post_install defines GCC_PREPROCESSOR_DEFINITIONS such as PERMISSION_CAMERA=1. The macros default to 0. In that case AudioVideoPermissionStrategy and LocationPermissionStrategy become empty subclasses of UnknownPermissionStrategy. Its status is Denied, and its request completes with PermanentlyDenied without calling AVFoundation or CoreLocation. No ios/Podfile is tracked, and none exists in the working tree. On the first iOS build, Flutter copies its template Podfile, whose post_install only calls flutter_additional_ios_build_settings. Swift Package Manager is off by default on stable, and this plugin version ships only a podspec. The app gates recording on these permissions: - ensureRequiredPermissions treats camera, microphone (when audio is on) and locationWhenInUse as required (app_permission_service.dart:78-88). - A blocking result stops bootstrap before camera and telemetry init (recording_screen.dart:97-112), and every START does the same (:368-381). - CameraProvider.initializeCamera also aborts on its own Permission.camera.request() (camera_provider.dart:372…
- **Failure scenario:** An iPhone build is made from a fresh clone, with the Podfile auto-generated from Flutter's template. 1. At first launch the audit reads Permission.camera.status and gets denied. It calls request() and gets permanentlyDenied. Microphone and Location behave the same way. 2. The rider sees 'Permissions Required - Recording cannot start until these permissions are granted: Camera, Microphone, Location ... Some permissions are permanently denied.' 3. No system prompt is ever shown, because the stub strategy never calls AVCaptureDevice.requestAccess or CLLocationManager.requestWhenInUseAuthorization. 4. 'Request Again' returns permanentlyDenied again (UnknownPermissionStrategy.m:19-21), and START re-runs the audit and returns. 5. Geolocator's own prompt is never reached either, because Telemetr…
- **Evidence:** - <pub-cache>/permission_handler_apple-9.4.7/ios/Classes/PermissionHandlerEnums.h:32-34 `#ifndef PERMISSION_CAMERA` / `#define PERMISSION_CAMERA 0`. Likewise :39-41 for PERMISSION_MICROPHONE and :67-69 for PERMISSION_LOCATION. - strategies/AudioVideoPermissionStrategy.h:9-19: `#if PERMISSION_CAMERA | PERMISSION_MICROPHONE ... #else #import "UnknownPermissionStrategy.h" @interface AudioVideoPermissionStrategy : UnknownPermissionStrategy`. strategies/LocationPermissionStrategy.h:9-21 uses the same pattern. - strategies/UnknownPermissionStrategy.m:11-13 `return PermissionStatusDenied;` and :19-21 `completionHandler(PermissionStatusPermanentlyDenied);`. - `git ls-files ios/` lists no Podfile. - <flutter>/packages/flutter_tools/lib/src/macos/cocoapods.dart:246-263 copies templates/cocoapods/Podfile-ios when no Podfile exists (:261 `podfileTemplate.copySync(podfile.path);`). Podfile-ios:39-43…
- **Verifier corrections:** Cited line numbers are slightly off: ensure() calls are at app_permission_service.dart:79-89 and the camera check at camera_provider.dart:372-386. Otherwise accurate. | - The finding's line numbers for the PERMISSION_CAMERA macro are shifted by about one line; PERMISSION_LOCATION is at :67-68. - PERMISSION_LOCATION_WHENINUSE is a valid macro in 9.4.7 (LocationPermissionStrategy.h:9), so the recommended define is correct.
- **Recommendation:** Commit ios/Podfile, generated from Flutter's Podfile-ios template. In post_install, after flutter_additional_ios_build_settings, append target.build_configurations GCC_PREPROCESSOR_DEFINITIONS ['$(inherited)', 'PERMISSION_CAMERA=1', 'PERMISSION_MICROPHONE=1', 'PERMISSION_LOCATION_WHENINUSE=1']. Add a line to README.md:161 saying iOS requires these Podfile macros.
- **Fix note:** ios/Podfile with permission_handler macros

#### F004

**Recording stops whenever MainActivity stops (screen timeout, lock, background without PiP): CameraX is bound to the Activity lifecycle and nothing keeps the screen on, contradicting the README/notification 'screen-off recording' claims**

Severity: **critical** · Category: lifecycle · Location: `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:614` · Verification: two-lens (code-truth + reachability/impact) → confirmed · Status: **deferred**

Also affects: `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/BackgroundRecordingService.kt:96`, `README.md:108`, `lib/features/recording/recording_screen.dart:294`, `lib/features/recording/recording_screen.dart:414`, `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/MainActivity.kt:25`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:638`, `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/motocam/MainActivity.kt:305`, `pub-cache camera_android_camerax-0.7.1/android/src/main/java/io/flutter/plugins/camerax/ProxyApiRegistrar.java:109` …

- **What is wrong:** controller.initialize() (614) creates the camera through camera_android_camerax 0.7.1. That plugin binds Preview and VideoCapture with its context as the LifecycleOwner, and the context is the Activity (CameraAndroidCameraxPlugin.java:53, ProxyApiRegistrar.java:109-116, ProcessCameraProviderProxyApi.java:68). FlutterActivity dispatches ON_STOP from onStop (engine FlutterActivity.java:212, 862-867). In CameraX 1.5.3, LifecycleCamera.onStop is annotated @OnLifecycleEvent(ON_STOP) and calls CameraUseCaseAdapter.detachUseCases() (confirmed with javap on the camera-lifecycle-1.5.3 classes.jar in the Gradle cache). So the camera closes whenever MainActivity is stopped. The persistent recording (the camera 0.12.0 default, camera_controller.dart:580-582) stays open but gets no video until the activity starts again. The foreground service and PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK keep the process and Dart timers alive, but they do not own the camera. Nothing keeps the activity visible: there is no FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON, keepScreenOn or wakelock anywhere in lib/, android/app/src/main or pubspec.yaml, so the system screen timeout turns the display off during a ride. Yet the FGS notification promises 'screen can b…
- **Failure scenario:** The rider mounts the phone, taps START and rides for an hour without touching it. After the system screen timeout (typically 30 s to 2 min) the display sleeps, MainActivity gets onStop, and CameraX detaches the use cases. Dart keeps rolling segments on its timers, but they hold no new video until the rider wakes the screen (a roll's restart may also wait on the closed camera). The rider finds only the first minute or so of the ride on video, and a crash later in the ride is not recorded. Blackout mode does not help, because it does not stop the screen timeout either.
- **Evidence:** ProxyApiRegistrar.java:109-116 `public LifecycleOwner getLifecycleOwner() { if (context instanceof LifecycleOwner) { return (LifecycleOwner) context; } ...`; CameraAndroidCameraxPlugin.java:53 `proxyApiRegistrar.setContext(activityPluginBinding.getActivity());`; FlutterActivity.java:212 `implements FlutterActivityAndFragmentDelegate.Host, LifecycleOwner`, :867 `lifecycle.handleLifecycleEvent(Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP);`; javap -c/-v androidx.camera.lifecycle.LifecycleCamera (camera-lifecycle 1.5.3, the version pinned by camera_android_camerax-0.7.1/android/build.gradle:75-79): `public void onStop(androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner)` annotated `androidx.lifecycle.OnLifecycleEvent(value=...ON_STOP)` and invoking `CameraUseCaseAdapter.detachUseCases:()V`; grep -i 'wakelock|keep_screen_on|keepScreenOn|FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON|setShowWhenLocked' over lib/, android/app/src/main and pubspec.yaml match…
- **Verifier corrections:** MainActivity.kt:305-308 also calls maybeEnterRecordingPip() from onPause. That does not help: PiP keeps the activity started only while the display is on, and screen-off still delivers onStop. The stall-recovery path (recording_screen.dart:231-268, recoverRecordingPipeline) runs only after resume, so it cannot recover the footage that was lost. | Narrow the scope. maybeEnterRecordingPip() runs from onPause and onUserLeaveHint (MainActivity.kt:300-327), so pressing Home while recording moves the Activity into PiP, where it stays STARTED and recording continues. The failure is specific to the screen turning off: the system screen timeout, or the power-button lock, which stops every activity,…
- **Recommendation:** In MainActivity.kt, call window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON) in the startForegroundRecording handler (where isRecordingActive becomes true) and clearFlags in stopForegroundRecording. Blackout mode can dim the screen instead of letting it sleep. Change BackgroundRecordingService.kt:96 and README.md:108 to say the screen must stay on, and that locking the phone pauses video. Real screen-off capture would need native CameraX bound to a LifecycleService inside the FGS; treat that as a separate project.
- **Fix note:** deferred by owner (decision 5) in the fix phase. Update 2026-10-28, owner-approved design: MainActivity overrides `lifecycle` with a mirror of FlutterActivity's registry that a hold keeps at STARTED while the recording's foreground service runs (engaged after a successful service start; released at stopForegroundRecording, a token-checked FOREGROUND_STOPPED broadcast from the service, and onDestroy), so CameraX keeps capturing with the screen off. New setting keep_screen_on_while_recording (default on) sets FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON during a recording as the fallback. The resume stall restart is skipped only when the in-progress REC*.mp4 demonstrably grew in the background. Docs, README and the notification text describe this; not yet confirmed on a device.

#### F005

**Once a session holds more locked segments than the loop size, the directory-wide retention sweep deletes the oldest videos of every ride, including locked incident clips, without any lock or session check**

Severity: **critical** · Category: correctness · Location: `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1193` · Verification: two-lens (code-truth + reachability/impact) → confirmed · Status: **fixed**

Also affects: `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1117-1124`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1157-1170`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1258-1298`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1300-1317`, `lib/features/history/rides_list_screen.dart:269`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1121`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1193`, `lib/features/camera/providers/camera_provider.dart:1220`

- **What is wrong:** The per-registration trim never removes locked entries (1118-1124), so locked segments pile up in _sessionSegmentPaths. Once a ride has more locked segments in total than _maxRollingSegments, the trim leaves the list above the limit and logs 'all segments are locked. Skipping overwrite for safety.' That is exactly the condition that disables the early return in _enforceRecordingRetention (1193-1198). The method then lists every .mp4/.mov/.mkv/.avi under the whole recordings directory, recursively (1200-1207). All rides share that one flat directory. It sorts the files by mtime and deletes the oldest (count - _maxRollingSegments) of them (1209-1234). Nothing checks the current session, the in-memory locks, or the lockedSegmentPaths persisted in any ride's sidecar. The sweep runs 2 s after every roll (1150-1151, 1157-1170) and twice per stop (1153, 1515). The stop-time passes also run _pruneOrphanTelemetryFiles (1253-1255, 1281-1293), which deletes the sidecars of rides whose videos were just removed, so those rides disappear from Ride History.
- **Failure scenario:** Loop buffer is 6, a selectable option (app_settings_provider.dart:61). During one ride, three incident events occur anywhere in the ride, from auto-crash or the LOCK button. Each event locks the 2 newest registered segments plus the next one (1305-1317), giving 7 or more locked segments. When the 7th locked segment registers, the deferred sweep runs 2 s later. It finds, e.g., 150 videos from earlier rides and deletes the 145 oldest: every earlier ride, including clips the rider locked. At STOP the prune then deletes those rides' .telemetry.json files. With the default loop of 24, the same happens after about 9 incidents in a long ride, or after repeated LOCK presses (each press adds a pending lock).
- **Evidence:** camera_provider.dart:1193-1198 `if (_sessionSegmentPaths.length <= _maxRollingSegments) { if (includeTelemetryPrune) { await _pruneOrphanTelemetryFiles(recordingsDir); } return; }`; 1201-1206 `await for (final entity in recordingsDir.list(recursive: true, followLinks: false)) { if (entity is! File || !_isVideoFilePath(entity.path)) { continue; } videoFiles.add(entity); }`; 1220-1227 `final removeCount = videoFiles.length - _maxRollingSegments; ... for (final file in videoFiles.take(removeCount)) { ... if (await file.exists()) { await file.delete();` (no lock check anywhere in 1180-1256); 1118-1123 `.indexWhere((path) => !_lockedSegmentPaths.contains(path)); if (removableIndex < 0) { _log('Loop limit exceeded, but all segments are locked. Skipping overwrite for safety.'); break; }`; 1150-1151 `if (deferMaintenance) { _queueDeferredMaintenance();`; 1515 `await _enforceRecordingRetention(i…
- **Verifier corrections:** The damage does not depend on earlier rides existing. With no other files in the directory, N+1 locked segments in the current session still make the sweep delete the oldest locked segment of the current ride. The sweep also runs from the non-deferred register at stop (1158-1160), which adds to the stop-time pass at 1515. | Two consequences make this worse than described. First, the sweep keeps only the N newest files across the whole directory, so if one session holds more than N locked segments, its own oldest locked clip(s) are deleted too, not only earlier rides. Second, at stop _enqueueDeferredGalleryExport (1338-1371) schedules the gallery drain 2 s later, but _enforceRecordingRetenti…
- **Recommendation:** In camera_provider.dart, remove the directory-wide deletion branch of _enforceRecordingRetention and keep only the orphan-telemetry prune. The per-session trim already enforces the loop, and the over-locked case should just log and keep the files. If a global cap is wanted later, implement it as deletion of whole unlocked rides that honours the lockedSegmentPaths in every sidecar. Add a unit test with N+1 locked segments plus files from another ride.
- **Fix note:** directory-wide sweep removed; locked footage never auto-deleted
